Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation

                                          rdfs:subClassOf onto:RecognizedEvent ;
                                          rdfs:comment "Missing dose event observation for PD Patient" ;
                                          rdfs:label "PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

                             owl:inverseOf onto:recordBelongsToPatient ;
                             rdfs:domain onto:Patient ;
                             rdfs:range onto:PersonalHealthRecord ;
                             rdfs:comment "patient has a health record"@en ;
                             rdfs:label "has Personal Health Record"@en .
							 
Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation

                                          rdfs:subClassOf onto:RecognizedEvent ;
                                          rdfs:comment "Missing dose event observation for PD Patient" ;
                                          rdfs:label "PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

###  http://schema.org/Person
schema:Person rdf:type owl:Class .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://www.case.edu/MDO#ActivitiesofDailyLiving

<http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto> rdf:type owl:Ontology ;
                                    owl:versionIRI <http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto/1.0.0> ;

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation
onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation rdf:type owl:Class ;
                                          rdfs:subClassOf onto:RecognizedEvent ;
                                          rdfs:comment "Missing dose event observation for PD Patient" ;
                                          rdfs:label "PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

###  http://schema.org/Person
schema:Person rdf:type owl:Class .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://www.case.edu/MDO#ActivitiesofDailyLiving
<http://www.case.edu/MDO#ActivitiesofDailyLiving> rdf:type owl:Class .


###  http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor

                                 rdfs:label "Tremor"@en .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

                   rdfs:domain onto:DailyDosagePlan ;
                   rdfs:range onto:DosageTime ;
                   rdfs:comment "the dosing time of a patient"@en ;
                   rdfs:label "has Dosage Time"@en .


###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#hasPersonalHealthRecord
onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord rdf:type owl:ObjectProperty ;
                             owl:inverseOf onto:recordBelongsToPatient ;
                             rdfs:domain onto:Patient ;
                             rdfs:range onto:PersonalHealthRecord ;
                             rdfs:comment "patient has a health record"@en ;
                             rdfs:label "has Personal Health Record"@en .
							 
Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation

                                          rdfs:subClassOf onto:RecognizedEvent ;
                                          rdfs:comment "Missing dose event observation for PD Patient" ;
                                          rdfs:label "PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

 ###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PD_Patient

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

###  http://schema.org/Person
schema:Person rdf:type owl:Class .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation

                                          rdfs:subClassOf onto:RecognizedEvent ;
                                          rdfs:comment "Missing dose event observation for PD Patient" ;
                                          rdfs:label "PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://www.case.edu/MDO#ActivitiesofDailyLiving

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limp, then that patient should be classified as a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
 onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

                   rdfs:subPropertyOf core:hasTime ;
                   rdfs:domain onto:DailyDosagePlan ;
                   rdfs:range onto:DosageTime ;

###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#hasPersonalHealthRecord
onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord rdf:type owl:ObjectProperty ;
                             owl:inverseOf onto:recordBelongsToPatient ;
                             rdfs:domain onto:Patient ;
                             rdfs:range onto:PersonalHealthRecord ;
                             rdfs:comment "patient has a health record"@en ;
                             rdfs:label "has Personal Health Record"@en .
							 
Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation

                                          rdfs:subClassOf onto:RecognizedEvent ;
                                          rdfs:comment "Missing dose event observation for PD Patient" ;
                                          rdfs:label "PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a daily dosage plan with a specific dosage time and a missing dose event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ hasDosageTime(?plan, ?time) ^ DosageTime(?time) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?event) ^ AlertingNotification(?notification) -> NotifyDoctor(?notification)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has tremor and bradykinesia of upper limb and performs a walking activity that is measured by a smart watch and a patient falling event is observed, then send a notification to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ <http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor>(?t) ^ <http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb>(?b) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?b) ^ onto:WalkingActivity(?a) ^ onto:performsActivity(?p, ?a) ^ onto:SmartWatch(?sw) ^ core:isMeasuredByDevice(?a, ?sw) ^ onto:PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?e) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?e, ?p) ^ onto:NotifyDoctor(?n) -> onto:sendNotification(?e, ?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a falling event observation and that observation happened 30 minutes after dosing, then send a notification alert to the doctor.
SWRL Rule:
 PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ obsAfterDosing(?obs, true) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has  tremor and that tremor is observed after a dosage time then a notification is sent to the relatives and a missing dose event observation is recognized.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?t) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:DosageTime(?dt) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?dt) ^ onto:hasSymptom(?p, ?t) ^ onto:observedAfter(?t, ?dt) ^ onto:NotifyRelatives(?n) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?e) -> onto:sentNotification(?p, ?n) ^ onto:hasRecognizedEvent(?p, ?e)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has bradykinesia of the upper limb and this observation  is made after dosing, then send a  notification to relatives and a missing dosing event observation is identified.
SWRL Rule:
onto:PD_Patient(?p) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:RecognizedEvent(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?brady) ^ sosa:observedProperty(?obs, ?brady) ^ sosa:resultTime(?obs, ?obsTime) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?doseTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(?obsTime, ?doseTime) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?notification) -> onto:NotifyRelatives(?notification) ^ onto:PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs)

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a falling event observation is identified for a patient with Parkinson disease, then send a notification alert to the nurse and the relative.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientFalllingEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ NotifyNurse(?n) ^ NotifyRelatives(?r) -> sendNotification(?n, true) ^ sendNotification(?r, true)

###  http://schema.org/Person
schema:Person rdf:type owl:Class .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a person is monitored and the low-level events of tremor and  bradykinesia of the upper limb are identified, and this person has a medical prescription that contains Dopamine, then it is most likely to be a patient with Parkinson disease.
SWRL Rule:
schema:Person(?p) ^ onto:hasTremor(?p, true) ^ onto:hasBradykinisiaOfUpperLimp(?p, true) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?mp) ^ onto:MedicalPrescription(?mp) ^ onto:hasSubstance(?mp, ?s) ^ swrlb:contains(?s, "Dopamine") -> onto:PD_Patient(?p)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
 ###  http://www.case.edu/MDO#ActivitiesofDailyLiving
<http://www.case.edu/MDO#ActivitiesofDailyLiving> rdf:type owl:Class .

<http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor> rdf:type owl:Class ;

                                 rdfs:label "Tremor"@en .

###  http://w3id.org/Wear4PDmove/onto#SketchingActivity

                                         owl:onProperty core:isMeasuredByDevice ;
                                         owl:someValuesFrom onto:SmartWatch

                       rdfs:comment "An activity followed by the patient during his free time, using his hands (sketching)."@en ;
                       rdfs:label "Sketching Activity"@en .

                rdfs:label "PD Patient" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a missing dosing event has been observed for a patient with Parkinson disease more than 2 times, then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
PD_Patient(?p) ^ PDpatientMissingDoseEventObservation(?obs) ^ sosa:hasFeatureOfInterest(?obs, ?p) ^ swrlb:greaterThan(count(?obs), 2) ^ AlertingNotification(?n) -> NotifyDoctor(?n)

                                          rdfs:comment "Missing dose event observation for PD Patient" ;
                                          rdfs:label "PD patient Missing Dose Event Observation" .

Rule in Natural Language:
  If a patient with Parkinson disease has a dose plan every 4 hours, and tremor and bradykinesia of the upper limb is identified within the boundaries of this interval then send notification to the doctor for action.
SWRL Rule:
onto:Patient(?p) ^ onto:ParkinsonDisease(?pd) ^ onto:hasDisease(?p, ?pd) ^ onto:hasPersonalHealthRecord(?p, ?plan) ^ onto:DailyDosagePlan(?plan) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?start) ^ onto:hasDosageTime(?plan, ?end) ^ onto:DosageTime(?start) ^ onto:DosageTime(?end) ^ swrlb:subtractDateTimesYieldingDayTimeDuration(?duration, ?end, ?start) ^ swrlb:equal(?duration, "PT4H"^^xsd:dayTimeDuration) ^ http://www.case.edu/MDO#Tremor(?tremor) ^ http://www.case.edu/PMDO#BradykinesiaofUpperLimb(?bradykinesia) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?tremor, ?p) ^ onto:identifiedForPatient(?bradykinesia, ?p) ^ core:hasTime(?tremor, ?tremorTime) ^ core:hasTime(?bradykinesia, ?bradykinesiaTime) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?tremorTime, ?end) ^ swrlb:greaterThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?start) ^ swrlb:lessThanOrEqual(?bradykinesiaTime, ?end) ^ onto:Doctor(?d) ^ onto:AlertingNotification(?n) -> onto:sendNotificationToDoctor(?n, ?d) ^ onto:notificationForPatient(?n, ?p)
RAG Content(Ontology Parts):
